# Playing for Time

Journal: `quests/main_quest/act_01/q101_resurrection` · `MainQuest` · hash `1336846915`.

Journal premise: Well, broder, looks like we got ourselves into a real fix. No chance in hell I'm wriggling myself out of this one, but you're still alive. And as Misty says, "As long as you're alive, there's hope." Fate's given you a second chance, so use it – get back on your feet. Do that, and consider your best friend's last wish fulfilled.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 121 exact journal-linked nodes in 17 resources; 21 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 18 content candidate, 3 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

Root quest resources with additional journal references: none indexed. Roots remain activation context when a phase/scene anchor exists, to avoid importing sibling launch scaffolding.

Generated structural analysis from decoded resources. Shared resources are context, not exclusive quest ownership. The tables trace stored sockets and fact dependencies; they do not simulate runtime ordering, prove all branches reachable, or infer chronology from journal objective order. These candidates are supported by the listed stored node types. They are reduction proposals, not implemented compiler types or claims that a node's presence proves a complete reusable lifecycle. Review topology, bindings and cancellation before promotion.

| `actor_route` | q101_01_covered_in_trash.scene / scene / 680; q101_08_takemura_v_room.scene / scene / 1001; q101_08_takemura_v_room.scene / scene / 1143; q101_08_takemura_v_room.scene / scene / 1285; +8 locations in catalogue.json | actor + authored route/spline + movement policy → movement output. World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

1186 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 364 distinct direct resource identities; 27 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.

## Scene performance events

Section event data retains actor/animation/audio bindings and authored time fields, with raw pointers. Events in different sections are not treated as one chronological timeline: parent routing, interruption and playback policy still apply. Animation/voice payloads and installed timing are not validated by this extraction.
